🧭 VLA (Vision-Language-Action)VLA (Vision-Language-Action)

· 发展脉络· Development

VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型旨在通过统一的多模态架构，使机器人能够理解自然语言指令并结合视觉感知直接生成控制动作，从而解决传统机器人系统泛化性差、任务依赖特定编程的痛点。该领域从早期的简单端到端策略迅速演进为具备世界模型、因果推理及分层规划能力的通用智能体，核心挑战从单纯的模仿学习转向了长程规划、物理常识理解及安全可靠性。发展脉络清晰地展示了从数据驱动的基础模型构建，到引入思维链（CoT）与强化学习优化，再到构建具身世界模型以实现零样本泛化和自我进化的完整过程。VLA (Vision-Language-Action) models aim to enable robots to understand natural language instructions and directly generate control actions by combining visual perception through a unified multimodal architecture, thereby addressing the pain points of poor generalization and task-specific programming dependence in traditional robotic systems. This field has rapidly evolved from early simple end-to-end policies to general agents possessing world models, causal reasoning, and hierarchical planning capabilities. The core challenges have shifted from purely imitation learning to long-horizon planning, physical commonsense understanding, and safety reliability. The development trajectory clearly demonstrates the complete process from data-driven foundation model construction, to introducing Chain-of-Thought (CoT) and reinforcement learning optimization, and finally to building embodied world models to achieve zero-shot generalization and self-evolution.

切入角度Perspectives

  • 架构解耦与分层控制Architecture Decoupling and Hierarchical Control
  • 动作表征与高效解码Action Representation and Efficient Decoding
  • 多模态感知增强与空间理解Multimodal perception enhancement and spatial understanding
  • 数据策略与合成数据Data Strategies and Synthetic Data
  • 推理机制与思维链Inference Mechanisms and Chain of Thought
  • 特定领域应用与微调Specific domain applications and fine-tuning
  • 安全与鲁棒性Safety and Robustness

· 各角度时间线· Timeline per Perspective

架构解耦与分层控制Architecture Decoupling and Hierarchical Control

该方向核心在于解耦认知规划与动作执行，以兼顾 VLA 模型的泛化能力与实时控制精度。早期工作如 RT-H 尝试引入语言中间层，但受限于推理延迟。随后，CogACT 和 Hi Robot 等通过分层架构（System 1/2）或专用扩散模块，将离散推理与连续控制彻底分离，显著提升了长程任务成功率。近期演进聚焦于异步执行机制（如 RTC、ACNet）与物理约束（如 PhysReflect），通过轻量级适配器或世界模型辅助，解决了大模型推理慢导致的动作抖动与物理不可行问题，实现了从“单一大模型”向“结构化协同系统”的范式转变。The core of this direction lies in decoupling cognitive planning from action execution to balance the generalization capabilities of VLA models with real-time control precision. Early work like RT-H attempted to introduce a language intermediate layer but was limited by inference latency. Subsequently, methods such as CogACT and Hi Robot utilized hierarchical architectures (System 1/2) or dedicated diffusion modules to completely separate discrete reasoning from continuous control, significantly improving success rates in long-horizon tasks. Recent evolution focuses on asynchronous execution mechanisms (e.g., RTC, ACNet) and physical constraints (e.g., PhysReflect), using lightweight adapters or world model assistance to solve issues of action jitter and physical infeasibility caused by slow large model inference, achieving a paradigm shift from a 'single large model' to a 'structured collaborative system'.

2026-09
Continuous Actions from Discrete Minds: Latent-Aligned Planning for End-to-End Autonomous Driving
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决传统VLA将离散语义强行映射至连续动作空间时产生的量化误差与推理延迟问题，以兼顾长程规划的语义一致性与实时控制的平滑性。Aiming to solve the quantization errors and inference latency issues arising when traditional VLA models forcibly map discrete semantics to continuous action spaces, thereby balancing semantic consistency in long-horizon planning with smoothness in real-time control.
方法: Method: 提出Latent-Aligned Planning框架，利用冻结的VQ-VAE解码器作为桥梁，在潜空间直接对齐离散规划与连续动作，避免迭代去噪过程。Proposes the Latent-Aligned Planning framework, which utilizes a frozen VQ-VAE decoder as a bridge to directly align discrete planning with continuous actions in latent space, avoiding iterative denoising processes.
推进: Advance: 证明了在特定物理先验下，连续潜空间预测比离散Token生成更稳健，显著降低了动作抖动并提升了长程任务的成功率。Demonstrates that under specific physical priors, continuous latent space prediction is more robust than discrete token generation, significantly reducing action jitter and improving success rates in long-horizon tasks.
2026-09
Drive-HWM: Hierarchical World Models for Dynamic-Latent Guided Autonomous Driving
动机: Motivation: 为了解决单一世界模型难以同时兼顾长程规划的低频需求与即时反应的高频精度，试图构建类似人类双系统的分层控制架构。Aims to address the difficulty of a single world model simultaneously meeting the low-frequency requirements of long-horizon planning and the high-frequency precision of immediate response, attempting to construct a hierarchical control architecture similar to the human dual-system.
方法: Method: 设计Drive-HWM分层世界模型，通过光流预测约束慢速模型的潜在空间以捕捉动态本质，并采用快速模型处理突发状况的即时修正。Designs the Drive-HWM hierarchical world model, which constrains the latent space of the slow model through optical flow prediction to capture dynamic essence, and employs a fast model to handle immediate corrections for unexpected situations.
推进: Advance: 实现了规划与执行的有效解耦，在保持长程任务成功率的同时，通过动态约束减少了无效计算，验证了分层架构在复杂动态环境中的可行性。Achieves effective decoupling of planning and execution, maintaining high success rates for long-horizon tasks while reducing invalid computations through dynamic constraints, validating the feasibility of hierarchical architectures in complex dynamic environments.
2026-09
HINT: Human-Intent Inception for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对长程操作任务中VLA模型因高频推理导致的认知过载与动作不连贯问题，探索通过外部意图提示降低推理频率的解耦策略。Addresses the cognitive overload and action incoherence caused by high-frequency inference in VLA models for long-horizon manipulation tasks, exploring decoupling strategies that reduce inference frequency through external intent prompts.
方法: Method: 引入HINT机制，通过外部人类意图头环与多视图路由，将连续的感知控制流解耦为低频的意图规划与高频的执行反馈。Introduces the HINT mechanism, which decouples continuous perception-control streams into low-frequency intent planning and high-frequency execution feedback via external human intent headbands and multi-view routing.
推进: Advance: 以零参数成本实现了推理频率的动态调整，证明了通过结构化提示而非扩大模型规模即可有效解决长程任务中的认知-执行错位问题。Achieves dynamic adjustment of inference frequency at zero parameter cost, demonstrating that structured prompts rather than scaling up model size can effectively solve the cognitive-execution misalignment problem in long-horizon tasks.
2026-09
Dense to MoE Adaptation for Compact Vision Language Action Policies
动机: Motivation: 为了解决大参数VLA模型在端侧部署时推理延迟高、算力受限的问题，试图解耦模型架构的稠密计算与稀疏执行机制。To address the high inference latency and limited computing power when deploying large-parameter VLA models on edge devices, this work attempts to decouple dense computation from sparse execution mechanisms in the model architecture.
方法: Method: 提出Dense2MoE适配框架，将稠密前馈网络转换为混合专家（MoE）结构，通过动态路由机制在推理时解耦并关闭不活跃的专家模块。It proposes the Dense2MoE adaptation framework, converting dense feedforward networks into a Mixture of Experts (MoE) structure, and decoupling and deactivating inactive expert modules during inference via a dynamic routing mechanism.
推进: Advance: 在大幅降低参数量的同时维持了高任务成功率，为VLA模型在资源受限环境下的分层部署提供了新路径，但也揭示了视觉骨干网络在架构转换中的敏感性。It maintains high task success rates while significantly reducing the parameter count, providing a new path for hierarchical deployment of VLA models in resource-constrained environments, while also revealing the sensitivity of visual backbone networks during architectural transformation.
2024-06
PerAct2: Benchmarking and Learning for Robotic Bimanual Manipulation Tasks
动机: Motivation: 现有双臂操作缺乏统一的基准与隐式协调机制，导致复杂任务中独立代理或固定层级策略难以兼顾效率与协作。Existing dual-arm manipulation lacks unified benchmarks and implicit coordination mechanisms, making it difficult for independent agents or fixed hierarchical policies to balance efficiency and collaboration in complex tasks.
方法: Method: 构建包含13个任务的双臂基准PerAct2，采用单网络行为克隆策略，通过共享编码器隐式学习双臂间的动作协调而非显式分层。Constructs the dual-arm benchmark PerAct2 comprising 13 tasks, employing a single-network behavior cloning strategy that implicitly learns inter-arm action coordination through a shared encoder rather than explicit hierarchical structuring.
推进: Advance: 证明了单网络架构在显存效率与隐式协调上的可行性，但揭示了纯端到端方法在处理高难度物理交互时的泛化瓶颈。Demonstrates the feasibility of single-network architectures in terms of memory efficiency and implicit coordination, but reveals generalization bottlenecks of pure end-to-end methods when handling high-difficulty physical interactions.
2024-07
Robotic Control via Embodied Chain-of-Thought Reasoning
动机: Motivation: 解决 VLA 模型泛化性差的问题，试图通过引入显式推理链（CoT）来增强规划能力。Address the poor generalization of VLA models by attempting to enhance planning capabilities through the introduction of explicit reasoning chains (CoT).
方法: Method: 提出 ECoT 框架，强制模型在输出动作前先进行包含坐标和动作的“思考”过程。Proposed the ECoT framework, forcing the model to undergo a 'thinking' process involving coordinates and actions before outputting actions.
推进: Advance: 显著提升了 OpenVLA 在复杂任务上的成功率，但依赖合成数据且推理速度大幅下降。Significantly improved success rates for OpenVLA on complex tasks, but relies on synthetic data and suffers from a drastic reduction in inference speed.
2024-11
CogACT: A Foundational Vision-Language-Action Model for Synergizing Cognition and Action in Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 离散化动作空间导致精度损失，需将认知推理与连续动作生成彻底解耦。Discretizing the action space leads to precision loss, requiring a complete decoupling of cognitive reasoning from continuous action generation.
方法: Method: 采用双模块架构，VLM 负责认知规划，专用扩散动作模块（DiT）负责连续动作生成。Adopt a dual-module architecture where the VLM handles cognitive planning and a dedicated diffusion action module (DiT) handles continuous action generation.
推进: Advance: 在真机实验中大幅超越 OpenVLA，证明了专用动作建模在精度和泛化性上的显著优势。Greatly surpassed OpenVLA in real-robot experiments, proving the significant advantages of dedicated action modeling in precision and generalization.
2024-12
NaVILA: Legged Robot Vision-Language-Action Model for Navigation
动机: Motivation: 避免大模型直接预测关节角度等“超纲”任务，需将推理与底层控制分离。Avoid having large models directly predict 'out-of-syllabus' tasks such as joint angles, requiring the separation of reasoning from underlying control.
方法: Method: 采用分层架构，VLM 负责高层推理，RL 策略负责底层导航执行，利用 LiDAR 数据。Adopt a hierarchical architecture where the VLM handles high-level reasoning and an RL policy handles low-level navigation execution, utilizing LiDAR data.
推进: Advance: 实现了从仿真到真机的稳健迁移，但计算开销大且对硬件依赖较高。Achieved robust transfer from simulation to reality, but with high computational overhead and significant hardware dependency.
2025-02
Hi Robot: Open-Ended Instruction Following with Hierarchical Vision-Language-Action Models
动机: Motivation: 单一模型难以同时处理高层语义理解与底层技能执行，需模拟人类双系统认知。A single model struggles to simultaneously handle high-level semantic understanding and low-level skill execution, requiring the simulation of human dual-system cognition.
方法: Method: 构建分层 VLA 模型，高层 System 2 负责规划，底层 System 1 负责执行，利用合成数据训练。Construct a hierarchical VLA model where the high-level System 2 handles planning and the low-level System 1 handles execution, trained using synthetic data.
推进: Advance: 实现了开放指令跟随，但底层技能库有限，泛化性仍受限于预定义技能片段。Achieved open-instruction following, but the low-level skill library is limited, and generalization remains constrained by predefined skill fragments.
2025-03
GR00T N1: An Open Foundation Model for Generalist Humanoid Robots
动机: Motivation: 平衡人形机器人的理解能力与实时控制需求，需模仿人类的双系统认知架构。Balance the understanding capabilities of humanoid robots with real-time control requirements, requiring the imitation of human dual-system cognitive architectures.
方法: Method: 采用双系统架构（System 2 推理 + System 1 动作），结合海量数据金字塔策略进行训练。Adopt a dual-system architecture (System 2 reasoning + System 1 action), combined with a massive data pyramid strategy for training.
推进: Advance: 在仿真到真机迁移上表现优异，但推理延迟仍是实时控制的瓶颈。Performs excellently in simulation-to-reality transfer, but inference latency remains a bottleneck for real-time control.
2025-06
WorldVLA: Towards Autoregressive Action World Model
动机: Motivation: 自回归生成动作序列时误差累积严重，需结合世界模型预测未来以修正动作。Error accumulation is severe when autoregressively generating action sequences, requiring the combination of world models to predict the future and correct actions.
方法: Method: 提出 WorldVLA，将世界模型与 VLA 结合，利用注意力掩码策略屏蔽历史动作误差。Proposed WorldVLA, combining world models with VLA and using attention masking strategies to shield historical action errors.
推进: Advance: 在 LIBERO 数据集上优于 OpenVLA，但自回归生成效率低且动作离散化仍有信息损失。Outperformed OpenVLA on the LIBERO dataset, but autoregressive generation efficiency is low, and action discretization still incurs information loss.
2025-06
Real-Time Execution of Action Chunking Flow Policies
动机: Motivation: 大模型推理延迟导致动作块生成卡顿，需实现异步思考与平滑执行。Large model inference latency causes stuttering in action block generation, requiring asynchronous thinking and smooth execution.
方法: Method: 提出 RTC 机制，将动作块生成视为图像修复，冻结已执行部分并平滑过渡剩余部分。Proposed the RTC mechanism, treating action block generation as image inpainting, freezing executed parts and smoothly transitioning the remaining parts.
推进: Advance: 在真实任务中容忍了 200ms 延迟，实现了稳如老狗的执行效果，但增加了计算开销。Tolerated 200ms latency in real-world tasks, achieving rock-solid execution performance, but at the cost of increased computational overhead.
2025-12
Motus: A Unified Latent Action World Model
动机: Motivation: 需统一 VLA、世界模型等多种架构以提升具身智能的通用性与性能。Need to unify VLA, world models, and other architectures to enhance the universality and performance of embodied intelligence.
方法: Method: 提出 Motus 架构，利用 MoT 和光流潜动作将仿真与真机性能统一，采用 Joint Attention 调度。Proposed the Motus architecture, utilizing MoT and optical flow latent actions to unify simulation and reality performance, employing Joint Attention scheduling.
推进: Advance: 仿真性能提升 45%，接近通用人形机器人基础模型，但光流计算对资源要求高。Simulation performance improved by 45%, approaching general-purpose humanoid robot foundation models, but optical flow computation imposes high resource requirements.
2026-01
Cosmos Policy: Fine-Tuning Video Models for Visuomotor Control and Planning
动机: Motivation: 利用视频生成模型的强大先验解决 VLA 的规划与控制问题，但需解决推理速度瓶颈。Leverage the powerful priors of video generation models to solve VLA planning and control problems, but need to address inference speed bottlenecks.
方法: Method: 将动作、状态和价值函数编码进视频扩散模型的潜在帧，直接微调视频模型。Encode actions, states, and value functions into the latent frames of video diffusion models and directly fine-tune the video model.
推进: Advance: 在仿真和真实任务中均获 SOTA，但 5 秒生成一个动作块的延迟限制了动态场景应用。Achieved SOTA in both simulation and real-world tasks, but the 5-second latency for generating one action block limits application in dynamic scenarios.
2026-01
Causal World Modeling for Robot Control
动机: Motivation: 解决 VLA 中视觉与动作纠缠导致的幻觉问题，需引入因果世界模型进行解耦。Address the hallucination problem caused by the entanglement of vision and actions in VLA, requiring the introduction of causal world models for decoupling.
方法: Method: 构建自回归流模型，结合异步推理和噪声历史增强，将视频预测与动作解码分离。Construct an autoregressive flow model, combining asynchronous reasoning and noise history enhancement to separate video prediction from action decoding.
推进: Advance: 数据效率高且解决了物理可行性问题，但双流设计增加了显存开销。High data efficiency and solved physical feasibility issues, but the dual-stream design increases memory overhead.
2026-06
Advancing Omnimodal Embodied Agents from Isolated Skills to Everyday Physical Autonomy
动机: Motivation: 单体模型在长程任务中脆弱，需通过规划、记忆、验证的解耦换取鲁棒性。Monolithic models are fragile in long-horizon tasks, requiring the exchange of robustness through the decoupling of planning, memory, and verification.
方法: Method: 提出分层异步架构，引入视觉预检机制作为“刹车片”，防止执行偏差。Proposed a hierarchical asynchronous architecture, introducing a visual pre-check mechanism as a 'brake' to prevent execution deviations.
推进: Advance: 显著提升了长程任务的鲁棒性，证明了结构化编排优于单纯模型缩放，但高层无法优化底层。Significantly improved robustness in long-horizon tasks, proving that structured orchestration outperforms simple model scaling, but the high level cannot optimize the low level.
2026-06
PhysReflect-VLA: Physical Feasibility and Self-Reflective Regulation for Reliable Vision-Language-Action Policies
动机: Motivation: VLA 模型常输出物理不可行的动作，需引入物理约束与自反思机制。VLA models often output physically infeasible actions, requiring the introduction of physical constraints and self-reflection mechanisms.
方法: Method: 外挂物理可行性评估模块与自反思大脑，形成闭环反馈以修正动作。The external physical feasibility assessment module and the self-reflective brain form a closed-loop feedback to correct actions.
推进: Advance: 在长程接触任务中显著提升了可靠性，但增加了推理延迟，不适合高速动态场景。Significantly improved reliability in long-horizon contact tasks, but increased inference latency, making it unsuitable for high-speed dynamic scenarios.
2026-06
Zero-Shot Long-Horizon Dexterous Manipulation via Multi-View 3D-Grounded VLM Reasoning
动机: Motivation: 端到端 VLA 泛化性差，需通过模块化思路将语义推理与物理执行解耦。End-to-end VLA has poor generalization, requiring a modular approach to decouple semantic reasoning from physical execution.
方法: Method: 利用多视图融合将 2D 关键点提升至 3D，结合原子动作库与现成运动规划器。Utilized multi-view fusion to lift 2D keypoints to 3D, combined with an atomic action library and off-the-shelf motion planners.
推进: Advance: 零样本下长程任务表现优异，但依赖 3D 重建精度且无法处理未见工具。Exhibited excellent performance in long-horizon tasks under zero-shot settings, but relies on 3D reconstruction accuracy and cannot handle unseen tools.
2026-06
PAMAE: Phase-Aware-MoE Action Experts Towards Reliable Flow-Matching Vision-Language-Action Policies
动机: Motivation: 单一专家模型难以适应机器人操作中不同阶段（如抓取、移动、放置）对动作分布的差异化需求，导致控制精度下降。Single expert models struggle to adapt to the differentiated action distribution requirements across different stages of robotic manipulation (e.g., grasping, moving, placing), leading to reduced control accuracy.
方法: Method: 提出PAMAE架构，基于阶段感知（Phase-Aware）机制将MoE专家路由与Flow-Matching结合，针对不同操作阶段动态调用专用专家。Proposes the PAMAE architecture, which combines MoE expert routing with Flow-Matching based on a Phase-Aware mechanism to dynamically invoke specialized experts for different manipulation stages.
推进: Advance: 验证了‘术业有专攻’的分层专家策略能显著提升长程任务成功率，但暴露了基于硬阈值阶段划分的脆弱性。Validates that a 'specialized expertise' hierarchical expert strategy significantly improves long-horizon task success rates, but exposes the fragility of hard-threshold-based stage partitioning.
2026-06
Co-VLA: Coordination-Aware Structured Action Modeling for Dual-Arm Vision-Language-Action Systems
动机: Motivation: 端到端VLA在紧密耦合的双臂协作中缺乏可解释的意图分解，导致隐式协调失效且动作平滑度难以动态调整。End-to-end VLAs lack interpretable intent decomposition in tightly coupled dual-arm collaboration, leading to implicit coordination failure and difficulty in dynamically adjusting action smoothness.
方法: Method: 设计Co-VLA结构化模型，利用SAE模块将动作显式解耦为共享意图与残差执行，并引入LAC控制器实时调节动作能量。Designs the Co-VLA structured model, utilizing an SAE module to explicitly decouple actions into shared intent and residual execution, and introduces a LAC controller to dynamically regulate action energy in real-time.
推进: Advance: 实现了从黑盒回归到白盒结构化控制的范式转变，通过显式解耦提升了双臂协作的透明度与物理可行性。Achieves a paradigm shift from black-box regression to white-box structured control, enhancing the transparency and physical feasibility of dual-arm collaboration through explicit decoupling.
2026-07
CoFL-S: Spatially Queryable Sector Flow Fields for Local Language-Conditioned Navigation
动机: Motivation: 高层规划剥离后，需解决低层动作执行的“最后一公里”问题。After decoupling high-level planning, the 'last mile' problem of low-level action execution needs to be addressed.
方法: Method: 提出流场（Flow Field）映射局部指令到连续控制，专注于局部稳健性。Proposed Flow Field mapping local instructions to continuous control, focusing on local robustness.
推进: Advance: 显著提升了抗碰撞能力，但假设局部子指令已知，无法独立解决长程规划。Significantly improved collision avoidance capabilities, but assumes local sub-instructions are known and cannot independently solve long-horizon planning.
2026-07
Teaching Vision-Language-Action Models What to See and Where to Look
动机: Motivation: VLA 注意力分散，需解耦“看”与“开”以增强空间 grounding。VLA attention is scattered, requiring the decoupling of 'seeing' and 'doing' to enhance spatial grounding.
方法: Method: 构建双模型架构，TGP-Prompter 负责视觉指引，TGP-Planner 负责规划，蒸馏视觉先验。Constructed a dual-model architecture where TGP-Prompter handles visual guidance and TGP-Planner handles planning, distilling visual priors.
推进: Advance: 解决了注意力分散痛点，在导航任务中表现优异，但依赖检测质量。Solved the attention scattering pain point, performing excellently in navigation tasks, but relies on detection quality.

方法: Method: 引入语义状态机作为事件驱动接口，仅在关键任务阶段转换时触发模型更新，强制机器人明确当前任务状态。Introducing a semantic state machine as an event-driven interface, triggering model updates only during key task phase transitions, forcing the robot to explicitly clarify its current task state.
推进: Advance: 通过事件驱动机制有效降低了推理频率，在无需预训练的情况下实现了语义与动作的精准对齐，提升了长程任务的逻辑连贯性。Effectively reducing inference frequency through an event-driven mechanism, achieving precise alignment between semantics and actions without pre-training, and improving logical coherence in long-horizon tasks.

RoboHarn-Evo: Evolving Hierarchical Physical Knowledge for Self-Improving Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 解决固定参数 VLA 模型在长周期任务中无法适应物理反馈变化及“知易行难”的僵化问题，探索不依赖参数微调的自我进化路径。Addressing the rigidity of fixed-parameter VLA models that cannot adapt to physical feedback changes and the 'knowing is easy, doing is hard' problem in long-cycle tasks, exploring a self-evolution path independent of parameter fine-tuning.
方法: Method: 将任务规划与动作执行解耦为两层知识，利用物理反馈不断修补外部“知识小本本”，实现经验蒸馏与自我迭代。Decoupling task planning and action execution into two layers of knowledge, continuously repairing external 'knowledge notebooks' using physical feedback to achieve experience distillation and self-iteration.
推进: Advance: 验证了在不更新基座模型参数前提下，通过外部知识修正机制可显著提升任务成功率，为分层控制提供了动态适应性的新范式。Verified that task success rates can be significantly improved through external knowledge correction mechanisms without updating base model parameters, providing a new paradigm for dynamic adaptability in hierarchical control.

Remember What You Did: Action-History Memory with Dual-Expert Denoising for Long-Horizon Vision-Language-Action Policies
动机: Motivation: 针对长程任务中因当前视觉信息模糊导致的动作规划歧义，解决单一帧感知无法维持任务连续性的问题。Addressing action planning ambiguity caused by vague current visual information in long-horizon tasks, solving the problem that single-frame perception cannot maintain task continuity.
方法: Method: 设计双专家去噪架构，利用轻量级 Mamba 编码动作历史作为先验指路，配合冻结基座进行动作精修。Designing a dual-expert denoising architecture, using a lightweight Mamba to encode action history as prior guidance, and cooperating with a frozen base for action refinement.
推进: Advance: 通过引入动作历史记忆模块，以极小的参数增量解决了感知歧义，证明了分层记忆机制在提升长程任务成功率上的工程有效性。By introducing an action history memory module, resolving perceptual ambiguity with minimal parameter increments, demonstrating the engineering effectiveness of hierarchical memory mechanisms in improving long-horizon task success rates.

动作表征与高效解码Action Representation and Efficient Decoding

该方向旨在解决 VLA 模型中自回归生成的延迟与扩散/流匹配的高计算成本之间的矛盾。核心思路从早期的连续动作直接预测，转向离散化分词（如 VQ、FAST）以利用 Transformer 的高效性，或引入隐式动作空间与流匹配（Flow Matching）提升生成质量与速度。演进路径表现为：从简单的动作分词（VQ-BeT）到频域优化（FAFM）和一致性蒸馏（Consistency Policy），再到利用潜空间先验（UniVLA, InternVLA）和推理时引导（OTQL, Q-Guided）。最新趋势聚焦于轻量化架构（SmolVLA, NORA）与无训练加速（Action Caching），试图在保持泛化能力的同时，实现端侧实时部署。This direction aims to resolve the contradiction between the latency of autoregressive generation in VLA models and the high computational cost of diffusion/flow matching. The core idea has shifted from early direct prediction of continuous actions to discrete tokenization (e.g., VQ, FAST) to leverage Transformer efficiency, or introducing implicit action spaces and flow matching (Flow Matching) to improve generation quality and speed. The evolutionary path manifests as: from simple action tokenization (VQ-BeT) to frequency domain optimization (FAFM) and consistency distillation (Consistency Policy), then to leveraging latent space priors (UniVLA, InternVLA) and inference-time guidance (OTQL, Q-Guided). The latest trends focus on lightweight architectures (SmolVLA, NORA) and training-free acceleration (Action Caching), attempting to achieve real-time edge deployment while maintaining generalization capabilities.

动机: Motivation: 旨在解决离散动作分词带来的量化误差与扩散模型高计算成本之间的矛盾，试图在保持连续动作精度的同时利用离散潜空间的高效性。Aims to resolve the contradiction between quantization errors caused by discrete action tokenization and the high computational cost of diffusion models, attempting to leverage the efficiency of discrete latent spaces while maintaining continuous action precision.
方法: Method: 提出一种潜空间对齐规划框架，利用冻结的 VQ-VAE 解码器将离散的语义潜变量映射回连续动作空间，实现端到端规划。Proposes a latent space aligned planning framework that utilizes a frozen VQ-VAE decoder to map discrete semantic latent variables back to the continuous action space, achieving end-to-end planning.
推进: Advance: 证明了在特定物理先验下，连续潜空间预测比强行离散化生成更稳健，显著降低了量化误差并提升了复杂场景下的成功率。Demonstrates that under specific physical priors, continuous latent space prediction is more robust than forcibly discretized generation, significantly reducing quantization errors and improving success rates in complex scenarios.

DIDO: Distilling Interaction-Centric Dynamics into One-Step Denoising for World Action Models
动机: Motivation: 为了解决扩散模型多步去噪带来的高计算成本与闭环控制延迟，试图压缩生成过程以实现实时动作预测。To address the high computational cost and closed-loop control latency caused by multi-step denoising in diffusion models, this work attempts to compress the generation process to achieve real-time action prediction.
方法: Method: 提出 DIDO 方法，通过蒸馏技术将多步去噪过程压缩为单步，并引入交互中心动态（Interaction-Centric Dynamics）与 DINOv3 对齐信号来弥补信息缺失。It proposes the DIDO method, compressing the multi-step denoising process into a single step via distillation technology, and introducing Interaction-Centric Dynamics and DINOv3 alignment signals to compensate for information loss.
推进: Advance: 在 LIBERO 任务上实现了 99.0% 的 SOTA 性能，证明了通过引入特定监督信号进行单步生成是平衡速度与精度的有效路径，为实时控制提供了新范式。It achieves 99.0% SOTA performance on LIBERO tasks, proving that single-step generation with specific supervision signals is an effective path to balance speed and accuracy, providing a new paradigm for real-time control.

方法: Method: 构建仅0.5B参数的SLIM模型，将动作与观测的交互直接学习在紧凑的潜在空间，完全摒弃了像素级解码和过度推理机制。Constructing the SLIM model with only 0.5B parameters, directly learning the interaction between actions and observations in a compact latent space, completely abandoning pixel-level decoding and over-thinking mechanisms.
推进: Advance: 在LIBERO等基准上以极小参数量超越7B+竞品，推理速度提升近6倍且显存减半，验证了紧凑潜在交互表征在保持泛化能力下的极致效率。Outperforming 7B+ competitors with minimal parameters on benchmarks like LIBERO, achieving nearly 6x faster inference speed and halved memory usage, validating the ultimate efficiency of compact latent interaction representations while maintaining generalization capabilities.

动机: Motivation: 解决传统GRPO算法在机器人强化学习中因全成功或全失败样本组被丢弃而导致的样本效率低下及训练浪费问题。To solve the low sample efficiency and training waste in robot reinforcement learning caused by the GRPO algorithm discarding entire groups of samples that are either all successful or all failed.
方法: Method: 设计轨迹质量分组策略，将具有相同结果的轨迹按质量细分，利用“分而治之”机制为失败轨迹保留梯度信号以优化策略。A trajectory quality grouping strategy is designed, subdividing trajectories with the same outcome by quality, utilizing a 'divide and conquer' mechanism to retain gradient signals for failed trajectories to optimize the policy.
推进: Advance: 突破了单纯依赖二元结果（成功/失败）的优化瓶颈，在保持无Critic网络优势的同时显著提升了样本利用率，但指出了依赖VLM打分可能引发的Reward Hacking风险。This breaks the optimization bottleneck of relying solely on binary outcomes (success/failure), significantly improving sample utilization while maintaining the advantage of no Critic network, but points out the risk of Reward Hacking potentially triggered by VLM scoring.

推进: Advance: 通过显式建模频谱异质性显著提升了特定任务下的生成效率与精度，但揭示了依赖训练集固定频谱策略在分布外任务中泛化能力下降的局限性。Significantly improves generation efficiency and precision under specific tasks by explicitly modeling spectral heterogeneity, but reveals the limitation of decreased generalization capability in out-of-distribution tasks when relying on fixed spectral strategies from the training set.

动机: Motivation: 旨在解决传统 VLA 模型利用点积注意力处理动作块时，无法有效捕捉动作中平滑趋势、高频修正及不同阶段间正交关系的缺陷。Aims to address the defect of traditional VLA models using dot-product attention to process action blocks, which fails to effectively capture smooth trends, high-frequency corrections, and orthogonal relationships between different stages in actions.

推进: Advance: 成功将几何代数中的楔积引入动作表征，在分布外（OOD）及真实机器人场景中显著增强了鲁棒性，证明了该机制在复杂动作分工下的有效性。Successfully introduces the wedge product from geometric algebra into action representation, significantly enhancing robustness in out-of-distribution (OOD) and real-robot scenarios, proving the effectiveness of this mechanism under complex action division of labor.

推进: Advance: 发现原论文中 CVAE 带来的性能提升实为超参数巧合，证明在默认设置下该模块是过度设计的累赘，为简化动作表征架构提供了关键的反直觉证据。It finds that the performance improvement brought by CVAE in the original paper was actually a hyperparameter coincidence, proving that the module is an over-designed burden under default settings, providing key counter-intuitive evidence for simplifying action representation architectures.

GeoAAC: Geometry-Based Adaptive Action Chunking from Denoising Trajectories in VLA Policies
动机: Motivation: 针对现有VLA策略采用固定时间跨度（Action Chunking）导致在精细操作阶段失效的问题，旨在利用去噪轨迹的几何特性实现自适应分块。Aiming to address the failure of existing VLA policies using fixed time spans (Action Chunking) during fine manipulation, we seek to achieve adaptive chunking by leveraging the geometric properties of denoised trajectories.
方法: Method: 从Flow Matching的去噪轨迹中提取速度场变化作为几何特征，以此动态调整动作分块的长度，无需额外训练即可判断预测可靠性。We extract velocity field changes from Flow Matching denoised trajectories as geometric features to dynamically adjust action chunk lengths, enabling reliability judgment of predictions without additional training.
推进: Advance: 提出了一种无训练的自适应机制，利用过程信息（几何特征）替代传统的熵估计，显著提升了实机操作成功率并解决了固定分块的僵化问题。We propose a training-free adaptive mechanism that utilizes process information (geometric features) to replace traditional entropy estimation, significantly improving real-robot operation success rates and solving the rigidity of fixed chunking.

Urgent Actions Go First: Urgency-Aware Denoising for Real-Time VLA Control
动机: Motivation: 针对 VLA 生成动作块时整体计算延迟高、无法匹配机器人执行“前紧后松”特性的矛盾，寻求在保持生成质量前提下的实时加速方案。Addressing the contradiction between high overall computational latency when VLA generates action blocks and the inability to match the robot's execution characteristic of 'tight front and loose back', seeking real-time acceleration solutions while maintaining generation quality.
方法: Method: 设计 Urgency-Aware Denoising (UAD) 策略，利用流匹配（Flow-matching）特性让关键的前序动作少迭代，后序动作多迭代，并引入“幽灵动作”补偿未执行轨迹。Designed an Urgency-Aware Denoising (UAD) strategy, utilizing Flow-matching characteristics to perform fewer iterations for critical preceding actions and more iterations for subsequent actions, and introducing 'ghost actions' to compensate for unexecuted trajectories.

多模态感知增强与空间理解Multimodal perception enhancement and spatial understanding

本方向旨在突破传统 VLA 模型仅依赖 2D 视觉的局限，通过引入深度、触觉、事件流及隐式空间表示等多模态感知，强化机器人对三维空间关系、物理接触反馈及长程时空上下文的理解。演进路径从早期的外挂深度图（如 SpatialBot）和触觉拼接（如 TLA），逐渐转向架构级的空间强制对齐（Spatial Forcing）与多模态语义融合（OmniVTLA）。核心思路在于将 3D 几何先验、力觉反馈及事件驱动记忆显式或隐式地注入 VLA 的表征空间，以解决长程规划中的幻觉问题及接触操作中的感知盲区，推动具身智能从“平面视觉”向“立体物理智能”跨越。This direction aims to break the limitations of traditional VLA models relying solely on 2D vision by introducing multimodal perception such as depth, touch, event streams, and implicit spatial representations, thereby strengthening robots' understanding of 3D spatial relationships, physical contact feedback, and long-range spatiotemporal context. The evolutionary path has shifted from early external depth maps (e.g., SpatialBot) and tactile stitching (e.g., TLA) towards architecture-level spatial forcing (Spatial Forcing) and multimodal semantic fusion (OmniVTLA). The core idea involves explicitly or implicitly injecting 3D geometric priors, force feedback, and event-driven memory into the VLA representation space to solve hallucination issues in long-horizon planning and perception blind spots in contact manipulation, promoting embodied intelligence from 'planar vision' to 'stereoscopic physical intelligence'.

动机: Motivation: 解决接触式操作中视觉感知盲区问题，试图通过引入触觉模态增强模型对物理接触的理解。Addresses visual perception blind spots in contact manipulation, attempting to enhance the model's understanding of physical contact by introducing the tactile modality.
方法: Method: 将触觉时序信息压缩为静态图，基于 Qwen2-VL 进行 LoRA 微调，构建触觉 - 语言 - 动作模型。Compresses tactile temporal information into static graphs, performs LoRA fine-tuning based on Qwen2-VL, and constructs a tactile-language-action model.
推进: Advance: 在仿真环境中实现了 85% 的成功率，证明了触觉信息对接触任务的重要性，但真机迁移和时序动态信息保留仍有局限。Achieves an 85% success rate in simulation environments, proving the importance of tactile information for contact tasks, but real-world transfer and retention of temporal dynamic information remain limited.

ForceVLA: Enhancing VLA Models with a Force-aware MoE for Contact-rich Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对现有 VLA 模型在复杂接触操作中缺乏力觉反馈的问题，试图将力觉作为一等模态融入架构。Addresses the lack of force feedback in existing VLA models during complex contact operations, attempting to integrate force as a first-class modality into the architecture.
方法: Method: 设计力感知混合专家（MoE）模块，动态融合视觉、语言与力信号，提升接触操作的物理智能。Designs a force-aware Mixture of Experts (MoE) module to dynamically fuse visual, language, and force signals, enhancing physical intelligence for contact manipulation.
推进: Advance: 在插拔任务中显著提升成功率，证明了力反馈对物理交互的必要性，但高度依赖昂贵传感器且泛化场景有限。Significantly improves success rates in plug-and-play tasks, proving the necessity of force feedback for physical interaction, but heavily relies on expensive sensors and has limited generalization scenarios.

动机: Motivation: 针对世界模型预测未来画面计算量大且易产生幻觉的问题，试图通过提取关键信息而非像素重建来优化空间预测。Aiming to solve the problem of world models having high computational costs for predicting future frames and being prone to hallucinations, this work attempts to optimize spatial prediction by extracting key information rather than pixel reconstruction.
方法: Method: 将未来预测降级为关键信息（如运动区域、深度）提取，砍掉预测头以节省算力，构建基于世界知识的 VLA。Downgrading future prediction to the extraction of key information (e.g., motion regions, depth), removing prediction heads to save compute, and constructing a VLA based on world knowledge.
推进: Advance: 在 CALVIN 数据集上表现优异，证明了关键信息提取比像素重建更高效，但对 RGB 依赖重且灵巧操作能力不足。Exhibits excellent performance on the CALVIN dataset, proving that key information extraction is more efficient than pixel reconstruction, yet it remains heavily dependent on RGB and lacks dexterous manipulation capabilities.

动机: Motivation: 针对 VLA 模型在物理预测中容易出现“像素复制陷阱”的问题，试图通过显式运动建模增强时空推理。Addressing the problem of VLA models easily falling into 'pixel copying traps' during physical prediction, this work attempts to enhance spatiotemporal reasoning through explicit motion modeling.
方法: Method: 引入视觉思维链（CoT），强制模型先计算光流再预测下一帧，统一 Tokenizer 设计以简化架构。Introducing visual Chain-of-Thought (CoT), forcing the model to first compute optical flow before predicting the next frame, and unifying Tokenizer design to simplify the architecture.
推进: Advance: 在 LIBERO 和真机上表现优异，证明了显式光流建模能有效提升物理预测的准确性和样本效率。Exhibits excellent performance on LIBERO and real robots, proving that explicit optical flow modeling effectively improves the accuracy and sample efficiency of physical prediction.

动机: Motivation: 解决 VLA 模型在长程操作中缺乏有效记忆、非马尔可夫问题突出的问题，试图通过事件驱动机制优化记忆存储。To address the lack of effective memory and prominent non-Markovian issues in VLA models during long-horizon operations, this work attempts to optimize memory storage through event-driven mechanisms.
方法: Method: 设计 KEM 模块，像预言家一样预测关键帧进行存储，而非无脑缓存，构建事件驱动的视觉证据记忆。Designing the KEM module to predict keyframes for storage like a prophet, rather than blindly caching, constructing event-driven visual evidence memory.

动机: Motivation: 旨在解决传统 VLA 模型缺乏触觉反馈导致的接触操作感知盲区，试图通过未来视觉监督机制让模型预判物理交互中的触觉变化。Aiming to solve the perception blind spots in contact operations caused by the lack of tactile feedback in traditional VLA models, attempting to enable models to predict tactile changes in physical interactions through future visual supervision mechanisms.
方法: Method: 提出 τ 架构，利用 JEPA 风格的自监督学习，仅通过双视角视觉监督而非像素重建来学习触觉增强的视觉 - 语言 - 动作表征。Proposing the τ architecture, which utilizes JEPA-style self-supervised learning to learn tactile-enhanced vision-language-action representations through dual-view visual supervision rather than pixel reconstruction.
推进: Advance: 实现了无需重建像素的动态触觉学习，显著提升了接触丰富任务的性能，揭示了多模态融合中单视角对齐优于双视角的规律。Achieving dynamic tactile learning without pixel reconstruction, significantly improving performance in contact-rich tasks, and revealing the pattern that single-view alignment outperforms dual-view alignment in multimodal fusion.

方法: Method: 采用物理运动检测锁定时间轴，结合视觉语言模型进行“看图说话”的分工式标注方案，规避纯视觉定位的误差。It adopts physical motion detection to lock the timeline, combining vision-language models for a 'describe the image' division-of-labor annotation scheme to avoid errors from pure visual localization.
推进: Advance: 验证了运动学信号与视觉语义融合在提升标注一致性上的工程价值，但也暴露了纯视觉加简单运动学在处理形变物体及接触力变化时的感知局限性。It verifies the engineering value of fusing kinematic signals with visual semantics in improving annotation consistency, but also exposes the perceptual limitations of pure vision combined with simple kinematics when handling deformable objects and contact force variations.

Opt2VLA: Force-Aware Vision-Language-Action for Contact-Rich Humanoid Whole-Body Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 克服现有 VLA 模型仅输出几何位姿而缺乏力觉反馈，导致在接触丰富任务中无法感知“用力过猛”或“力度不足”的缺陷。It overcomes the defect of existing VLA models that only output geometric poses without force feedback, leading to an inability to perceive 'too much force' or 'insufficient force' in contact-rich tasks.
方法: Method: 将力控从黑盒中解耦，直接让 VLA 预测力参考值，利用优化器生成数据训练模型实现力觉感知的端到端输出。It decouples force control from the black box, directly having VLA predict force reference values, and uses an optimizer to generate data for training the model to achieve end-to-end output of force perception.
推进: Advance: 实现了从“看山是山”到“摸山知力”的跨越，证明了在 VLA 表征空间显式注入力觉反馈能显著提升全身操作任务中的物理交互能力。It achieves a leap from 'seeing the mountain as a mountain' to 'touching the mountain to know the force', proving that explicitly injecting force feedback into the VLA representation space can significantly enhance physical interaction capabilities in whole-body manipulation tasks.

EgoAlign: Bridging the Human-Humanoid Gap for Long-Range Loco-Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对传统遥操作数据获取成本高且难以覆盖长程时空上下文的问题，旨在突破人类经验向人形机器人身体映射时的感知与动作鸿沟。Addressing the high cost of acquiring traditional teleoperation data and the difficulty in covering long-horizon spatiotemporal contexts, aiming to bridge the perception and action gap when mapping human experience to humanoid robot bodies.
方法: Method: 利用仿真器与特定控制器（SONIC）将人类视频动作翻译为机器人可理解的 Token，实现无需真机训练的直接策略迁移。Utilizing simulators and specific controllers (SONIC) to translate human video actions into robot-understandable tokens, achieving direct policy transfer without real-robot training.
推进: Advance: 成功将人类长程交互经验注入机器人表征空间，实现了 10 米搬运等长程任务，证明了低成本获取多模态时空数据的有效性。Successfully injected human long-horizon interaction experience into the robot representation space, achieving long-horizon tasks such as 10-meter carrying, proving the effectiveness of low-cost acquisition of multimodal spatiotemporal data.

RawVLA: Embodied Neural Image Signal Processor For Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 指出传统 VLA 模型依赖固定 ISP 处理图像会丢失关键物理信号，导致机器人在极端光照下丧失对三维环境的感知能力。Pointing out that traditional VLA models relying on fixed ISP processing for images lose key physical signals, causing robots to lose perception of 3D environments under extreme lighting conditions.
方法: Method: 引入轻量级神经图像信号处理器（ISP），在冻结 VLA 策略前对 RAW 数据进行自适应增强，恢复被丢弃的底层视觉特征。Introducing a lightweight neural image signal processor (ISP) to adaptively enhance RAW data before freezing the VLA policy, recovering discarded low-level visual features.
推进: Advance: 将图像质量从审美问题转化为任务成功的关键因素，显著提升了机器人在暗光等复杂物理环境下的空间感知鲁棒性。Transforming image quality from an aesthetic issue into a key factor for task success, significantly improving the spatial perception robustness of robots in complex physical environments such as low light.
2026-09
Seek Before You Move: Evidence Seeking for Progress Grounding in Vision-Language Navigation
动机: Motivation: 解决现有 VLN 模型因缺乏显式空间验证机制而产生的“进展近视症”，即在不确定的空间环境中盲目行动导致的幻觉与迷路。Addressing the 'progress myopia' in existing VLN models caused by the lack of explicit spatial verification mechanisms, i.e., hallucinations and getting lost due to blind action in uncertain spatial environments.
方法: Method: 提出“先寻找证据再行动”的主动探索策略，强制模型在关键决策点暂停并采集多视角空间证据以 grounding 当前状态。Proposing an active exploration strategy of 'seek evidence before acting', forcing the model to pause at key decision points and collect multi-view spatial evidence to ground the current state.
推进: Advance: 通过显式引入空间验证机制，将被动推理转化为主动的空间上下文构建，有效降低了长程导航中的空间认知错误率。By explicitly introducing a spatial verification mechanism, transforming passive reasoning into active spatial context construction, effectively reducing spatial cognitive error rates in long-horizon navigation.

EgoHumanoid-V2: Human-to-Humanoid Transfer of Coordinated Whole-Body Skills for Loco-Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决人形机器人在全身协调操作中，因视角受限（缺乏 egocentric 感知）和平衡控制缺失导致的具身空间理解偏差。Aiming to address embodied spatial understanding biases in humanoid robots caused by limited viewing angles (lack of egocentric perception) and missing balance control during whole-body coordinated operations.
方法: Method: 构建基于 egocentric 视角的全身协调技能转移框架，通过离线对齐与多次仿真 rollout 将人类经验映射到机器人本体。Constructing a whole-body coordinated skill transfer framework based on egocentric perspective, mapping human experience to the robot embodiment through offline alignment and multiple simulation rollouts.
推进: Advance: 首次实现了从人类视角到机器人视角的全身空间感知与动作对齐，突破了单一肢体操作局限，增强了复杂物理接触下的空间理解。For the first time achieving whole-body spatial perception and action alignment from human perspective to robot perspective, breaking through the limitations of single-limb operation and enhancing spatial understanding under complex physical contact.

数据策略与合成数据Data Strategies and Synthetic Data

VLA 领域正经历从依赖昂贵真人演示向大规模合成与无监督数据策略的范式转移。核心思路是利用互联网视频、强化学习生成、仿真模拟及世界模型预训练，突破物理交互数据的稀缺瓶颈。演进路径上，早期工作如 LAPA 尝试从视频提取潜动作，随后 GR-2 与 GraspVLA 验证了亿级合成/网络数据对泛化的提升；近期研究如 RLDG 与 π0.5 引入 RL 蒸馏与跨实体知识迁移，而 HABIT 与 VLK 则聚焦于构建包含人类交互与高保真物理接触的合成数据集。这一趋势旨在通过数据规模效应与合成技术的进步，降低真机采集成本，实现低成本、高泛化的具身智能。The VLA field is undergoing a paradigm shift from relying on expensive human demonstrations to large-scale synthetic and unsupervised data strategies. The core idea leverages internet videos, reinforcement learning generation, simulation, and world model pre-training to break through the scarcity bottleneck of physical interaction data. In terms of evolution, early work like LAPA attempted to extract latent actions from videos, followed by GR-2 and GraspVLA validating the generalization improvement from hundreds of millions of synthetic/network data points; recent studies like RLDG and π0.5 introduce RL distillation and cross-entity knowledge transfer, while HABIT and VLK focus on constructing synthetic datasets containing human interaction and high-fidelity physical contact. This trend aims to reduce real-world data collection costs and achieve low-cost, high-generalization embodied intelligence through data scale effects and advances in synthetic technology.

2026-09
ZETA: A Controlled Study of Zero-Shot Cross-Embodiment VLA Transfer for Tabletop Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对跨实体零样本迁移中缺乏系统性归因分析的问题，试图量化不同数据策略（如预训练暴露与数据多样性）对泛化性能的具体贡献。Addresses the lack of systematic attribution analysis in cross-entity zero-shot transfer, attempting to quantify the specific contributions of different data strategies (such as pre-training exposure and data diversity) to generalization performance.
方法: Method: 设计了严格的控制变量基准测试，隔离表示、多样性及辅助任务等因子，通过少量目标域数据预训练来评估VLA模型的跨实体迁移能力。Designs a rigorous controlled variable benchmark test, isolating factors such as representation, diversity, and auxiliary tasks, and evaluating the cross-entity transfer capabilities of VLA models through pre-training with a small amount of target domain data.
推进: Advance: 量化发现仅需5%的目标域数据预训练即可带来显著性能提升，澄清了仿真到现实迁移中非单调现象的成因，为低成本数据策略提供了理论依据。Quantitatively finds that pre-training with only 5% of target domain data can bring significant performance improvements, clarifying the causes of non-monotonic phenomena in sim-to-real transfer and providing a theoretical basis for low-cost data strategies.

Scaling Bimanual Household Manipulation from 1,500 hours of Demonstrations to On-Policy Corrections
动机: Motivation: 旨在验证海量多源数据（特别是互联网视频与手持设备数据）的规模效应能否突破双臂操作任务的瓶颈，并解决真机采集成本高昂的问题。Aims to verify whether the scale effect of massive multi-source data (particularly internet videos and handheld device data) can break through the bottlenecks of bimanual manipulation tasks and solve the problem of high costs in real-robot data collection.

推进: Advance: 实证了数据清洗与混合策略对提升折叠衣物等任务成功率的关键作用，揭示了互联网视频数据在弥补真机数据稀缺性上的巨大潜力。Empirically demonstrates the critical role of data cleaning and mixing strategies in improving success rates for tasks such as folding clothes, revealing the immense potential of internet video data in compensating for the scarcity of real-robot data.
2024-10
Latent Action Pretraining from Videos
动机: Motivation: 真人演示数据稀缺且采集成本高昂，限制了 VLA 模型的大规模预训练。Scarcity and high acquisition costs of human demonstration data limit large-scale pre-training of VLA models.
方法: Method: 提出 Latent Action Pretraining (LAPA)，将视频帧间变化量化为离散潜动作 token，利用无监督方式预训练 VLM 预测这些 token，再微调映射至真实动作。Proposes Latent Action Pretraining (LAPA), quantifying inter-frame video changes into discrete latent action tokens, pre-training VLMs to predict these tokens in an unsupervised manner, and then fine-tuning to map to real actions.
推进: Advance: 成功将预训练成本降低 30 倍，并证明了互联网视频可作为有效的机器人预训练数据源，虽未完全摆脱真数据依赖，但大幅降低了门槛。Successfully reduces pre-training costs by 30 times and proves internet videos can serve as effective robot pre-training data sources, though not fullyeliminating reliance on real data, it significantly lowers the threshold.

GR-2: A Generative Video-Language-Action Model with Web-Scale Knowledge for Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在未见物体和复杂任务上泛化能力不足，需更大规模数据支撑。Existing VLA models lack sufficient generalization capabilities on unseen objects and complex tasks, requiring larger-scale data support.
方法: Method: 构建 GR-2 模型，利用 3800 万网络视频和 500 亿 token 进行预训练，并引入视频生成作为规划器辅助动作预测。Constructs the GR-2 model, pre-training with 38 million internet videos and 50 billion tokens, and introducing video generation as a planner to assist action prediction.
推进: Advance: 验证了数据规模对性能的显著正向作用，在百余个任务上达到 97.7% 成功率，但揭示了纯视频生成在物理动态模拟上的局限性。Validates the significant positive impact of data scale on performance, achieving a 97.7% success rate on over a hundred tasks, yet reveals limitations of pure video generation in simulating physical dynamics.

RLDG: Robotic Generalist Policy Distillation via Reinforcement Learning
动机: Motivation: 人类演示数据存在噪声和不一致，直接微调大模型易导致灾难性遗忘。Human demonstration data contains noise and inconsistencies, and direct fine-tuning of large models easily leads to catastrophic forgetting.
方法: Method: 提出 RLDG，利用强化学习（RL）生成的高精度动作分布数据来蒸馏机器人基础模型，替代部分人类演示。Proposes RLDG, utilizing high-precision action distribution data generated by reinforcement learning (RL) to distill robot foundation models, replacing part of human demonstrations.
推进: Advance: 展示了 RL 生成数据在弥补人类演示粗糙度方面的潜力，但受限于奖励函数设计的难度及 RL 策略可能产生的“抄近道”行为。Demonstrates the potential of RL-generated data in compensating for the roughness of human demonstrations, yet is limited by the difficulty of reward function design and potential 'shortcut' behaviors in RL policies.

方法: Method: π0.5 采用“混血训练”策略，利用 97.6% 非目标机器人数据通过跨实体、跨模态知识蒸馏实现技能迁移。π0.5 adopts a 'mixed-blood training' strategy, utilizing 97.6% non-target robot data to achieve skill transfer through cross-entity and cross-modal knowledge distillation.

动机: Motivation: 单纯动作监督导致 VLA 模型存在“监督赤字”，难以理解物理规律。Pure action supervision leads to a 'supervision deficit' in VLA models, making it difficult to understand physical laws.
方法: Method: DriveVLA-W0 引入世界模型预测未来画面，提供稠密自监督信号，放大数据缩放定律的效果。DriveVLA-W0 introduces world model prediction of future frames to provide dense self-supervised signals, amplifying the effects of data scaling laws.

Human-as-Humanoid: Enabling Zero-Shot Humanoid Learning from Ego-Exo Human Videos with Human-Aligned Embodiments
动机: Motivation: 人形机器人缺乏足量的人类动作数据，且直接映射存在关节运动学失真。Humanoid robots lack sufficient human motion data, and direct mapping introduces kinematic distortions in joint movements.
方法: Method: Human-as-Humanoid 利用 Ego-Exo 人类视频，通过逆运动学（IK）和正向运动学（FK）约束将人类动作映射到人形机器人指令。Human-as-Humanoid utilizes Ego-Exo human videos, employing inverse kinematics (IK) and forward kinematics (FK) constraints to map human actions to humanoid robot commands.
推进: Advance: 实现了从人类视频到人形机器人的零样本动作迁移，通过分阶段约束减少了运动失真，但泛化性仍受限于特定硬件与接触任务。It achieves zero-shot action transfer from human videos to humanoid robots, reducing motion distortion through phased constraints, though generalization remains limited by specific hardware and contact tasks.

动机: Motivation: VLA 模型对环境变化（如摄像头视角）敏感，缺乏自适应能力。VLA models are sensitive to environmental changes (e.g., camera viewpoints) and lack adaptive capabilities.
方法: Method: ICWM 提出 In-Context World Modeling，让机器人通过“试探”动作隐式构建世界模型，无需微调即可适应新环境。ICWM proposes In-Context World Modeling, allowing robots to implicitly construct world models through 'probe' actions, adapting to new environments without fine-tuning.
推进: Advance: 将世界模型从显式训练转为隐式推理，实现了无需参数更新的环境适应，但随机试探在真实物理世界存在安全风险。It shifts world modeling from explicit training to implicit reasoning, achieving environment adaptation without parameter updates, though random probing poses safety risks in the real physical world.

Domain Arithmetic: One-Shot VLA Adaptation under Environmental Shifts
动机: Motivation: VLA 模型在环境发生显著偏移时性能急剧下降，需低成本适配方案。VLA model performance drops sharply when the environment undergoes significant shifts, necessitating low-cost adaptation solutions.
方法: Method: Domain Arithmetic 提出“向量加减法”，通过提取源域与目标域的权重差值作为“域向量”进行单样本微调。Domain Arithmetic proposes 'vector addition and subtraction,' extracting weight differences between source and target domains as 'domain vectors' for single-sample fine-tuning.
推进: Advance: 提供了一种高效的单样本环境适配方法，减少了重训需求，但在极端视角偏移下性能仍不稳定。It offers an efficient single-sample environment adaptation method, reducing retraining needs, though performance remains unstable under extreme viewpoint shifts.

Unleashing More Actions via Action Compositional Training for VLA Models
动机: Motivation: VLA 模型在未见物体组合的 OOD 任务上表现不佳，缺乏组合泛化能力。VLA models perform poorly on OOD tasks involving unseen object combinations, lacking compositional generalization capabilities.
方法: Method: Action Compositional Training 利用合成数据将推理技巧前置到训练阶段，通过技能串联增强模型泛化性。Action Compositional Training leverages synthetic data to front-load reasoning skills into the training phase, enhancing model generalization through skill chaining.

推进: Advance: 突破了离线数据利用的瓶颈，证明了在推理阶段无法获取未来信息的情况下，通过蒸馏机制挖掘历史轨迹中的潜在因果关联，能显著提升移动 GUI 代理在复杂任务中的泛化能力。Breaks through the bottleneck of offline data utilization, proving that even when future information is unavailable during inference, distillation mechanisms can mine potential causal associations in historical trajectories, significantly enhancing the generalization capability of mobile GUI agents in complex tasks.

推理机制与思维链Inference Mechanisms and Chain of Thought

该方向旨在解决 VLA 模型在复杂任务中缺乏可解释性与长程规划能力的痛点，核心思路是将“思考”显式化，通过引入视觉或文本形式的思维链（CoT）、世界模型预测及中间规划步骤，实现“先想后做”。演进脉络从早期简单的文本 CoT 辅助（如#10, #15），逐渐发展为融合视觉潜规划（#57）、光流运动推理（#58）及自适应快慢思考机制（#54）。近期研究更强调通过强化学习对齐推理过程（#61, #68），验证物理一致性（#133），并探索测试时推理扩展（#81），试图在保持实时性的同时提升模型在分布外场景的泛化与鲁棒性。This direction aims to address the pain points of VLA models lacking interpretability and long-horizon planning capabilities in complex tasks. The core idea is to make 'thinking' explicit by introducing visual or textual Chain of Thought (CoT), world model predictions, and intermediate planning steps to achieve 'think before act'. The evolution has progressed from early simple text CoT assistance (e.g., #10, #15) to integrating visual latent planning (#57), optical flow motion reasoning (#58), and adaptive fast-slow thinking mechanisms (#54). Recent research emphasizes aligning the reasoning process via reinforcement learning (#61, #68), verifying physical consistency (#133), and exploring test-time inference scaling (#81), attempting to improve generalization and robustness in out-of-distribution scenarios while maintaining real-time performance.

方法: Method: 利用 RL 对 VLA 进行后训练，通过奖励信号引导模型发现如“推切”等复杂操作逻辑。It utilizes RL for post-training VLA, guiding the model to discover complex operational logics such as 'push-cut' through reward signals.
推进: Advance: 证明了 RL 能显著提升 LIBERO 任务表现并发现新策略，但严重依赖仿真环境，Sim-to-Real 仍存在差距。It proves that RL can significantly improve LIBERO task performance and discover new strategies, but it heavily relies on simulation environments, and Sim-to-Real gaps remain.

方法: Method: 提出 DeepThinkVLA，采用混合注意力与两阶段训练，利用 RL 奖励强制 CoT 成为有效规划。It proposes DeepThinkVLA, adopting mixed attention and two-stage training, using RL rewards to force CoT into effective planning.

GroundControl: Anticipating Navigation Failures in Vision-Language Agents via Trajectory-Consistent Uncertainty Estimates
动机: Motivation: 试图解决 VLN 代理“看似自信实则迷路”的问题，通过监控轨迹动态变化来提前预警失败。It attempts to solve the problem of VLN agents 'appearing confident but actually getting lost' by monitoring dynamic trajectory changes to provide early failure warnings.
方法: Method: 提出 GroundControl，利用卡尔曼滤波融合轨迹特征与距离目标信号，识别振荡或停滞。It proposes GroundControl, utilizing Kalman filtering to fuse trajectory features with distance-to-target signals to identify oscillations or stagnation.
推进: Advance: 比单纯看 token 置信度更可靠，但严重依赖精确位置信息，且本质为事后诊断而非在线干预。It is more reliable than simply looking at token confidence, but heavily relies on precise location information and is essentially a post-hoc diagnosis rather than online intervention.

Seek Before You Move: Evidence Seeking for Progress Grounding in Vision-Language Navigation
动机: Motivation: 针对现有 VLA 模型在导航任务中普遍存在的“进展近视症”，即关键证据不足时盲目行动导致推理不可靠的问题。Addressing the prevalent 'progress myopia' in existing VLA models for navigation tasks, where blind actions are taken when evidence is insufficient, leading to unreliable reasoning.
方法: Method: 提出 SeekVLN 框架，通过引入“先找证据再行动”的主动探索机制，强制模型在不确定时暂停并执行视觉搜索以获取进展依据。We propose the SeekVLN framework, which introduces an active exploration mechanism of 'seek evidence before acting', forcing the model to pause and execute visual search to acquire progress evidence when uncertain.
推进: Advance: 将被动推理转化为主动规划，显著提升了复杂路口场景下的任务成功率，验证了显式证据寻求对长程规划鲁棒性的关键作用。Transforming passive reasoning into active planning significantly improves task success rates in complex intersection scenarios, validating the critical role of explicit evidence seeking in long-horizon planning robustness.

Taming VLAs under Robot Execution Errors: Self-Compensation and Stress Testing
动机: Motivation: 解决 VLA 模型在真实物理环境中因执行误差（如摩擦、背隙）导致推理与执行脱节，缺乏自我修正能力的痛点。Addressing the pain point that VLA models lack self-correction capabilities due to execution errors (e.g., friction, backlash) in real physical environments, causing a disconnect between reasoning and execution.
方法: Method: 利用本体感知残差在线微调 LoRA 模块，构建 RoboStress 基准进行压力测试，实现基于物理噪声的实时自我补偿。We utilize proprioceptive residual online fine-tuning of LoRA modules, construct the RoboStress benchmark for stress testing, and achieve real-time self-compensation based on physical noise.
推进: Advance: 在无需复杂强化学习对齐的情况下，通过简单的残差学习显著提升了模型在分布外物理噪声下的执行鲁棒性，验证了在线微调对推理一致性的价值。Without requiring complex reinforcement learning alignment, simple residual learning significantly improves model execution robustness under out-of-distribution physical noise, validating the value of online fine-tuning for reasoning consistency.

特定领域应用与微调Specific domain applications and fine-tuning

本方向聚焦于 VLA 模型在 GUI 操作、自动驾驶及特定机器人本体的垂直领域应用与微调。核心动机在于解决通用大模型在特定任务中泛化差、推理慢及物理交互不准的问题。演进路径从早期的闭源大参数模型（如 RT-2）转向开源轻量化架构（OpenVLA），并迅速向“数据高效”与“强化学习微调”转型。具体方法上，通过 LoRA 适配、动作空间离散化/连续化优化、以及引入 RL 进行策略蒸馏，显著提升了特定场景下的成功率与实时性。同时，针对 GUI 和驾驶场景，提出了纯视觉解析、语言 - 动作对齐及世界模型生成等创新策略，旨在填补仿真到真实的鸿沟，实现从“通用基座”到“领域专家”的落地跨越。This direction focuses on vertical domain applications and fine-tuning of VLA models in GUI operation, autonomous driving, and specific robot bodies. The core motivation is to address the poor generalization, slow reasoning, and inaccurate physical interaction of general large models in specific tasks. The evolutionary path has shifted from early closed-source large-parameter models (e.g., RT-2) to open-source lightweight architectures (OpenVLA), rapidly transitioning towards 'data efficiency' and 'reinforcement learning fine-tuning'. In terms of specific methods, LoRA adaptation, action space discretization/continuous optimization, and introducing RL for policy distillation have significantly improved success rates and real-time performance in specific scenarios. Meanwhile, for GUI and driving scenarios, innovative strategies such as pure visual parsing, language-action alignment, and world model generation have been proposed to bridge the Sim2Real gap, achieving the leap from 'general foundation' to 'domain expert' deployment.

方法: Method: 提出 ShowUI，采用 UI 引导的 Token 选择策略压缩输入，在保留位置信息的同时提升训练速度，并尝试在线 RL 优化。Proposing ShowUI, which adopts a UI-guided token selection strategy to compress inputs, improving training speed while preserving positional information, and attempting online RL optimization.
推进: Advance: 2B 小模型在 Screenspot 达到 75.1% 准确率，证明了轻量化模型在纯视觉 GUI 任务中的竞争力，但 Zero-shot 能力仍逊于离线大模型。A 2B small model achieves 75.1% accuracy on Screenspot, proving the competitiveness of lightweight models in pure visual GUI tasks, yet zero-shot capabilities still lag behind offline large models.

安全与鲁棒性Safety and Robustness

在 VLA 安全与鲁棒性视角下，研究重心正从传统的提示词注入转向物理世界与模型底层的深层脆弱性。早期工作多关注逻辑层面的对抗，而近期研究开始揭示架构差异（如回归头与 Flow 头）对位翻转攻击的敏感性，并尝试构建包含物理语义环境的分层信任边界。演进路径显示，防御策略正从单纯的仿真测试向量化完整性保护及物理可达性验证过渡，核心挑战在于平衡大规模模型中局部权重保护的成本与真实物理环境下的闭环鲁棒性，旨在填补仿真与真实世界验证之间的巨大鸿沟。From the perspective of VLA safety and robustness, the research focus is shifting from traditional prompt injection to deep vulnerabilities in the physical world and model foundations. Early work mostly focused on logical-level adversarial attacks, while recent studies begin to reveal the sensitivity of architectural differences (e.g., regression heads vs. Flow heads) to bit-flip attacks and attempt to construct hierarchical trust boundaries containing physical semantic environments. The evolutionary path shows that defense strategies are transitioning from simple simulation testing to vector integrity protection and physical reachability verification. The core challenge lies in balancing the cost of local weight protection in large-scale models with closed-loop robustness in real physical environments, aiming to fill the huge gap between simulation and real-world verification.

LIBERO-VPro: Benchmarking Closed-Loop Visual Robustness of Robotic Foundation Models
动机: Motivation: 针对现有 VLA 模型在闭环交互中因视觉遮挡或视角冲突导致的脆弱性，揭示其依赖空间先验而非真实视觉内容的本质缺陷。Address the fragility of existing VLA models in closed-loop interactions caused by visual occlusion or viewpoint conflicts, revealing their essential flaw of relying on spatial priors rather than real visual content.
方法: Method: 构建 LIBERO-VPro 基准，通过 12 类视觉挑战在仿真与少量真实环境中测试，量化交互线索对模型鲁棒性的影响。Construct the LIBERO-VPro benchmark, testing across 12 types of visual challenges in simulation and a few real environments, quantifying the impact of interaction cues on model robustness.
推进: Advance: 发现 WAM 等模型在递归反馈下极易崩盘，证明当前世界模型在闭环中仅是“瞎猜”，并指出训练数据分布局限是主因而非架构缺陷。Found that models like WAM are extremely prone to collapse under recursive feedback, proving that current world models are merely 'guessing blindly' in closed loops, and pointing out that training data distribution limitations are the main cause rather than architectural defects.
2026-09
ActiveArena: Benchmarking and Understanding Active Perception in Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 解决具身 AI 在固定视角下训练导致长程记忆缺失及动态环境泛化能力差的问题，打破“温室”训练模式。Solve the problem of missing long-term memory and poor generalization in dynamic environments caused by training embodied AI under fixed viewpoints, breaking the 'greenhouse' training mode.
方法: Method: 提出 ActiveArena 基准，引入可动头颈强制机器人进行主动感知搜索，并对比不同记忆采样策略的鲁棒性。Propose the ActiveArena benchmark, introducing movable heads and necks to force robots into active perception search, and comparing the robustness of different memory sampling strategies.
推进: Advance: 证实简单分块记忆比复杂子任务触发更鲁棒，量化了 ID 与 OOD 场景下的性能差距，揭示了主动感知对提升泛化的关键价值。Confirmed that simple chunked memory is more robust than complex sub-task triggering, quantified the performance gap between ID and OOD scenarios, and revealed the critical value of active perception in improving generalization.
2026-09
CARE: Experience-Guided Atomic Corrective Execution for Vision-Language-Action Policies
动机: Motivation: 应对 VLA 策略在执行偏离名义轨迹时的脆弱性，试图将执行失败数据转化为提升模型鲁棒性的训练素材。Address the fragility of VLA policies when executing deviations from nominal trajectories, attempting to convert execution failure data into training material to enhance model robustness.
方法: Method: 设计 CARE 框架，将任务拆解为原子阶段，利用 3D 监控实时检测偏差并触发微调或重执行，构建经验引导的原子修正机制。Design the CARE framework, decomposing tasks into atomic stages, using 3D monitoring to detect deviations in real-time and trigger fine-tuning or re-execution, building an experience-guided atomic correction mechanism.
推进: Advance: 提出“后悔药”机制，通过数据合成避免人工扰动的主观性，虽填补了执行修正基准空白，但也暴露了复杂光照下对 3D 监控的依赖瓶颈。Propose the 'regret pill' mechanism, using data synthesis to avoid the subjectivity of manual disturbance; while filling the gap in execution correction benchmarks, it also exposes the bottleneck of reliance on 3D monitoring under complex lighting conditions.
2026-09
Faster and Better? Benchmark Bugs and Design Limitations Distort the Evaluation of Vision-Language-Action Acceleration
动机: Motivation: 现有基准测试因代码缺陷和物理引擎失真导致评估结果虚假繁荣，掩盖了VLA模型在真实物理环境下的脆弱性。Existing benchmarks suffer from false prosperity in evaluation results due to code defects and physics engine distortions, masking the fragility of VLA models in real physical environments.
方法: Method: 系统性地审查并修复了22个基准测试Bug及4个设计缺陷，重构了评估流程以还原模型真实性能。Systematically reviewing and fixing 22 benchmark bugs and 4 design flaws, reconstructing the evaluation process to restore the model's true performance.
推进: Advance: 揭示了“唯成功率论”的评估幻觉，证明了修复后的基准能更真实地反映模型在物理交互中的鲁棒性边界。Revealing the evaluation illusion of 'success rate only', proving that the repaired benchmarks can more authentically reflect the robustness boundaries of models in physical interaction.
2026-09
FP2: Equipping Robotic Foundation Models with Force Control
动机: Motivation: 机器人基础模型缺乏对物理接触力的感知能力，导致在“摸爬滚打”任务中极易发生碰撞或操作失败。Robot foundation models lack perception of physical contact forces, leading to frequent collisions or operation failures in 'crawling and rolling' tasks.
方法: Method: 提出FP2架构，通过外挂轻量级“小脑”模块专门处理力控，与主模型的路径规划能力解耦。Proposing the FP2 architecture, which uses an external lightweight 'cerebellum' module to specifically handle force control, decoupled from the main model's path planning capabilities.
推进: Advance: 在不改变主模型架构的前提下，显著提升了模型在物理接触任务中的安全性，填补了纯视觉语言模型在力觉反馈上的缺失。Significantly improving model safety in physical contact tasks without changing the main model architecture, filling the gap in force feedback for pure vision-language models.
2026-09
Taming VLAs under Robot Execution Errors: Self-Compensation and Stress Testing
动机: Motivation: VLA模型在部署时面临摩擦、背隙等物理噪声导致的执行误差，现有方法难以在动态环境中实时补偿。VLA models face execution errors caused by physical noise such as friction and backlash during deployment, where existing methods struggle to compensate in real-time within dynamic environments.
方法: Method: 利用本体感知残差在线微调LoRA，并构建RoboStress基准测试模拟真实的物理噪声环境。Utilizing proprioceptive residual online fine-tuning of LoRA and constructing the RoboStress benchmark to simulate real physical noise environments.
推进: Advance: 验证了基于本体感知的自我补偿机制能有效提升物理环境下的闭环鲁棒性，为应对真实世界不确定性提供了务实方案。Validating that the proprioception-based self-compensation mechanism can effectively improve closed-loop robustness in physical environments, providing a pragmatic solution for coping with real-world uncertainties.
2026-09
Disentangling Spurious Correlations in Vision-Language-Action Models via Predicting Domain-Invariant Latent Lookahead
动机: Motivation: VLA模型在仿真中表现优异但在现实中失效，往往是因为依赖背景等虚假相关性而非真正的物理语义理解。VLA models perform well in simulation but fail in reality, often because they rely on spurious correlations like background rather than true physical semantic understanding.
方法: Method: 提出DILL方法，通过预测去除背景噪音后的未来状态来解耦视觉特征与任务逻辑，强制模型学习因果关联。Proposing the DILL method, which decouples visual features from task logic by predicting future states after removing background noise, forcing the model to learn causal associations.
推进: Advance: 显著提升了模型在分布偏移场景下的泛化能力，揭示了通过合成数据多样性对抗虚假相关性是提升物理世界鲁棒性的有效路径。Significantly improving the model's generalization ability in distribution shift scenarios, revealing that combating spurious correlations through synthetic data diversity is an effective path to improving robustness in the physical world.
